ANCHORED REPORTS (M4★, ALIAS-UNIFY-1 eleventh review): a report a pass can make only LATER — parameter detection's check of a dot fallback whose miss implicit-argument resolution decides (`Expr.DotCall.ProvisionalMiss`) — takes the place the deferring pass reserved for it (`DiagnosticBag.ReserveAnchor`, made later under `InsertingAt`): after every report made before the anchor, before every report made after it, anchors reserved at one point in reservation order and the reports of one anchor in their own order. The list is exactly the list the operation would have produced had each anchored report been made at its anchor, and the prefix law holds for it: an anchored report that moves the last stored one past N drops it (the marker takes its severity), and one whose place is past N is dropped unformatted. Counters count a report when it is made, so no decision between the deferring and the deciding pass reads one (the front-end pipeline gates on no count before resolution). OBSERVATIONAL IRRELEVANCE: every decision reads the bag's counters, never its stored list — clause-head recovery (`ReportedCount`; the lexer hands the parser `lexicalErrorStarts`, recorded for every lexical error whether stored or not, replacing the former binary search over stored lexical diagnostics), the module cache (a module is cached only when `ReportedErrorCount` did not move across its nested walk), failed-module-parse classification (`HasReported(NestingTooDeep | AstDepthLimitExceeded)` on the module's own bag), and materialization success (`HasReportedErrors`) — so the recovery tree, downloads, caching, classification, and evaluation gating are identical at every capacity. Processing continues after exhaustion: its remaining work is the linear source traversal the source-length ceilings already bound (measured 85–590 bytes per code unit past the limit — tokens and the recovery tree — against about 1.8 KB for valid source), and stopping early would have made downloads and the editor's recovery tree depend on the budget. STAGES: a WITHHELD list — the passes of a provisional elaboration (a missing module), whose diagnostics may depend on the missing members — goes to a `CreateStage()` bag of the same capacity, so it never stores more than one list could, and it is never committed. (Until Q-04, September 2026, parameter detection and implicit-argument resolution also reported into stages that the ownership-completion pass could discard and replace, committed by `DiagnosticBag.AddRange(DiagnosticBag)`; that pass is deleted, every pass of a normal elaboration or materialization reports directly, and the commit path is deleted with it.) A loaded module's own parse (summarized as one diagnostic at its load site) uses a bag of the run's capacity. BOUNDED PAYLOAD: a longer message keeps a surrogate-safe prefix of up to `DiagnosticBag.MaxRetainedMessageLength` (4096) UTF-16 code units followed by `…`. A stored message is decided by its first `DiagnosticBag.RetainedPrefixLength` (4097) units alone — whether it is cut, and where the surrogate-safe cut falls — so the transparent-alias wrapper suggestion of `OutputSpreadMarkerOnAlias` writes only that raw prefix (`Rendering.TextPrefixBuilder`). Its form comes from `CallableSignature.HasStructuralParameterPattern`, recorded once for a signature snapshot and shared through a lifted template's tail. The written form of a parameter shown by `UnforwardableParameter` and `ExplicitParametersRequireOutput` takes only the 513 units its name bound reads (`ExprNameRenderer.BoundDisplayName`, the walk of `ParameterPattern.DisplayName` stopped there). The stored text, marker and line layout are the whole message's; measured per stored report (2026-11-23), the wrapper over 100,000 parameters went from 14.2 MB to 28 KB and the written form of a 1,000,000-unit name from 4 MB to 15 KB. Whether the body binds such a parameter in another form — the `UnforwardableParameter` wording "in that form" — is read from its leaves in `ParameterPattern.CapturesExpandingSharedNodesOnce` order, a shared node's once, only until one decides (`ParameterPattern.AnyCaptureExpandingSharedNodesOnce`, one index cursor per open group), never from a list of every leaf: measured per stored report (2026-11-24, Release), a 100,000-leaf parameter went from 4.2 MB to 12 KB (16 KB when no leaf decides, whose wording is longer), and the check itself over a group of leaves from 4.2 MB to no allocation. Text a diagnostic repeats PER REFERENCE is bounded where it is formatted, reading only what it shows: the parameter list of an open target that requires arguments (`ExprNameRenderer.BoundedJoin` over the lazy `ParameterPattern.EnumerateCaptures`, at most 512 declarations examined), the family name of a conditional-branch undeclared identifier (`ExprNameRenderer.BoundName`), and the names of a blocked strict-value forwarding (a diagnostic Q-15 removed on 2026-10-02) — each had been quadratic (32 KB of source rendered 162 million characters). LINEAR WORK BEHIND THE REPORTS: bounding what a list keeps does not bound the work of deciding what to report, so three analyses a flood drove super-linearly are linear, each pinned by a deterministic traversal observation: parameter detection finds the first written span of EVERY undeclared name of a closed body or branch in ONE walk (`ParameterDetector.FindFirstResolveSpans`, `DetectorSpanSearchExpansions`; a walk per name was quadratic — 200 KB of `u{i} + 1` rows took 11 s, now 0.44 s); implicit-argument resolution judges closed-list forwarding ONCE per callee pattern list and region (`ResolverWalkMemos.MissingForwardingNames`, deduplicated through a set, `ResolverForwardingVerdicts`; judged per reference, twice, with a list-scan dedup it was cubic — 64 KB of `abs(G)` references to a wide `G` took 215 s, now 0.25 s); and the parameter/property collision validator memoizes a node's visited contexts by an INTERNED context id (`ParameterPropertyCollisionValidator.ContextId`: the name-set content key is hashed once per bindings instance, `CollisionContextInterns`; hashing it at every node made one algorithm of P parameters quadratic, valid programs included — 524 KB of `G = v0, v1, …` took 6.8 s, now 0.48 s). Bare forwarding builds no argument after a row's first unforwardable parameter and stops there when there is no diagnostic list. Once the list is full, a failed contract consisting of ordinary named parameters can count its distinct missing names from the smaller caller's bindings. The run indexes the callee names once (a composed signature shares its tail index), and the bag counts the dropped errors in bulk. Other contracts retain the ordered per-parameter check. Only stored reports ask whether the body binds a parameter in another form (`BareForwardingVerdicts`, `BareForwardingIndexSteps`, `BareForwardingArgumentsBuilt`, `UnforwardableParameterWordingChecks`; reports made and messages built: `UnforwardableParameterReports`, `UnforwardableParameterMessages`, `OutputSpreadMarkerOnAliasReports`, `OutputSpreadMarkerOnAliasMessages`; what a stored message read: `OutputSpreadMarkerWrapperParameterReads`, `UnforwardableParameterDisplayNodes`). The public `Lexer.Tokenize` keeps every token and bounds its diagnostic list at the supported maximum; a source over `SourceProcessingLimits.MaxSupportedSourceLength` is rejected up front with an `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` (SEC-F1), never tokenized partially. Downstream renderers — `KatLangException.Message`, `RunResult` display, the CLI `check` loop, a materialization failure's joined message, a host's serializer — are bounded by the list, so no renderer carries a cap of its own. Measured after, at 2 MiB: 1,001 diagnostics and 0.1–0.2 MB retained for lexer and parser floods, exception text at most 199,970 characters. Hosts detect truncation by the code, never by the message. `DiagnosticBudgetTests`, `SourceProcessingLimitsTests`, `RootStrayCloserRecoveryTests`, `ModuleLoadingSecurityTests`, `KatLangErrorCodeTests`, the CLI `DiagnosticFlood_PrintsOneBoundedList`.

## One coordinate space per result

Every source span exposed in the result of processing a document — a `Diagnostic.Span`, an `EvalError.Span`, a `KatLangError.Span`, a semantic-model site — belongs to THAT document's coordinate space (September 2026, public API cleanup Task 3a). `SourceSpan` carries no source identity, so this is the only way a span can be meaningful: a module a document loads is parsed as its own document by the loader (`Parser.ParseSyntax` on the fetched text, positioned in the MODULE's text), but the tree that crosses into the loading document's result is the module's IMPORT VIEW — the same declarations with every source location removed (`ModuleLoader.ToImportView`, the ONE boundary: applied once per fetched module, after the module's own structural preflight and before its nested loads are elaborated or it enters the per-URL cache, so every splice — first or cached, eager or deferred — shares one caller-independent locationless instance; `Expr.Span`, `DotCall.MemberSpan`, `SequenceSpread.SpreadMarkerSpan`, `Property.DeclarationSpans`, parameter and collect-marker spans, and binder-pattern spans are cleared, while declaration identity, deferred-region and provenance slots, deconstruction metadata, exposure, and the written-list bit are carried through the record copy, sharing preserved and a spanless subtree returned as the same instance). The module's OWN parse outcomes never leave the loader in module coordinates: a parse error is one `InvalidLoadedSource` at the load site, and a non-error module parse diagnostic is re-anchored at the load site under its `[while loading url]` prefix. A construct inside the view is then reported at a location the CURRENT document supplies, never at a module-relative coordinate and never at a fabricated sentinel: (1) evaluation errors through the existing attach-if-missing law (`Evaluator.AtSpanIfMissing` / `WithSpan`: an error keeps its own span, acquires the enclosing expression's span only when it has none, and stays spanless when no enclosing expression has one), which positions a module runtime error at the demanding local expression — the reference, dot call, or call the document wrote — for direct, nested, chained-module, and deferred-branch demands alike; (2) loader diagnostics for a load written INSIDE a module (a failed fetch, a cycle, a policy or budget rejection, a nested parse failure) at the IMPORT SITE — the span, in the current document, of the outermost load directive through which that content was demanded (`ModuleLoader._importSite`, part of the swappable walk context; a deferred region records the site its body was reached under, `DeferredModuleRegion.ImportSite`, and its materialization restores it), while the spliced node itself keeps only the load call's own span; (3) front-end diagnostics raised against imported content — a declaration collision, an undeclared identifier in a closed list or branch, a refused open provider, an ineligible Grace marker, a parameter-owned open head, an unforwardable parameter or callable, an invalid inferred signature, a written ambiguous open name (and, until Q-15 removed it on 2026-10-02, a refused strict-value forwarding) — at the import site the pass reached the view through, ONCE per module unit at its first import site (Q-32 I-U: one module declaration is elaborated once, however many sites import it) (`ImportSite.OfBlock` for `load('…')` / `open '…'` in expression position, `ImportSite.OfProperty` — the declaring name — for a property whose direct value load elaboration made the module; a nested view inherits the enclosing site; every pass tracks the site as walk state — `DetectionRun.ImportSite`, `ResolutionRun.ImportSite`, the validators' field — consults it exactly where a diagnostic's own span is absent, and a demand-time pass over a deferred body starts from the region's recorded site); (4) provenance notes (`ImplicitParameterProvenance.Span`) for a promotion inside imported content are simply spanless, so the rendered message states the inference without a coordinate while keeping the receiver-aware wording and suggestion (the former `CanPositionAtOrigin` in-module flag is gone), and `ParameterPropertyCollision` names a parameter's position only when the document wrote it. Local diagnostics are positioned exactly as before: the rules above act only where a span is ABSENT. Consequences: no production code keys `SourceSpan` by reference identity (the validators dedup by declaration/target NODE; the semantic model's by-reference module span set is gone — see `editor-semantics.md`), which is what let `SourceSpan` become a value type (Task 3b, next section) without any semantic change; structural rejections of a spanless node are positioned at the nearest positioned ancestor on the preflight's path (`AstStructuralPreflight.SpanOf(node, frames, frameCount)`); and because spans decide NOTHING about elaboration, the hoisted deconstruction right-hand side carries its written row index structurally (`Algorithm.User.AssignmentDeconstructionRowIndex`, merged by `AstHelpers.WrittenRows`), so a module's implicit-parameter order survives its locationless import. NOT modeled: no source identity (`SourceId`, source table, cross-file navigation into module text). Lean has no counterpart: source locations are C# diagnostic metadata. `ModuleCoordinateSpaceTests`, `ModuleLoaderTests`, `BranchLazyModuleLoadingTests`, `SemanticModelTests` (`ModuleProvenance_*`).

The import-site anchor survives module-root conversion to a callable alias. `ImportSite.OfProperty` and `OfBlock` read `Algorithm.IsModuleRoot`, covering both user and alias roots. Later declaration and open validators must retain this anchor even when the imported tree has no spans; otherwise they can mistake invalid imported source for a spanless host-built tree and skip validation. `ModuleLawPostCommitReviewTests` pins eager and selected deferred imports, property and expression edges, and local diagnostic positions.

## Explicit value-type source locations

A location is either a real half-open source span or it is absent (September 2026, public API cleanup Task 3b). `SourcePosition` is a `readonly record struct` of a 1-based `Line` and a 1-based `Column` (UTF-16 code units from the start of the line, the lexer's contract: `\n` ends a line, `\r` advances nothing, a surrogate pair is two columns), ordered lexicographically by line then column (`IComparable<SourcePosition>`, the comparison operators, `Min`/`Max`); its constructor rejects any coordinate below 1. `SourceSpan` is a `readonly record struct` of two positions, the HALF-OPEN range `[Start, End)`: `Start` is the first covered coordinate and `End` the first coordinate AFTER the covered text, so `[1:1, 1:2)` covers one code unit, `[1:1, 1:1)` is a valid EMPTY span (an insertion point: the end-of-input diagnostic, a missing token, the parser's zero-length recovery marker), and the invariant is `Start <= End` — an inverted range or a zero coordinate is an `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` at construction, whether built from two positions or from four integers. `Contains(position)` is `Start <= p < End` (start included, end excluded, an empty span contains nothing, two spans sharing a boundary never both contain it, multi-line ranges compare lexicographically), `Union` is the hull (min start, max end; an empty operand still extends the hull to its position), equality and hashing are by value, and there is no reference identity anywhere: nothing keys a location by object. `ToString` renders `[1:1, 1:6)` and an empty span as `[1:6]`; the user-facing `KatLangError.ToString` (`[line:column] message`) and every provenance rendering print the START coordinate only, so half-open ends never surface as an inclusive-looking display. ABSENCE IS `null`: every location that can genuinely be missing is a `SourceSpan?` — `Diagnostic.Span` (a whole-document limit such as `SourceLengthExceeded`, `AggregateSourceLengthExceeded`, or `ModuleElaborationStackExhausted`, and a diagnostic against imported content with no import site, are unpositioned), `EvalError.Span`, `KatLangError.Span`, every AST span (`Expr.Span`, `DotCall.MemberSpan`, `SequenceSpread.SpreadMarkerSpan`, parameter and collect-marker spans, binder `NameSpan`s; a collection such as `Property.DeclarationSpans` holds real spans only and spells absence as emptiness), `ImplicitParameterProvenance.Span`, the import sites — and no sentinel coordinate exists any more (the former `(1,1,1,1)` / `(0,0,0,0)` fallbacks are gone; a real span at line 1 column 1 is a real location). The struct's `default` value (all coordinates 0) is NOT absence and NOT a location: the constructors refuse it, `SourceSpanValidator` reports it, and no production code reads a default span as meaningful — a `SourceSpan?` is the only spelling of "no location". ABSENCE MUST NEVER BE *MANUFACTURED* INTO A LOCATION (F2, September 2026): because the element type is a value type, a default-returning query over a collection of spans — `FirstOrDefault`, `SingleOrDefault`, `LastOrDefault`, or any generic helper instantiated at `SourceSpan` — turns emptiness into `default(SourceSpan)`, which the surrounding `SourceSpan?` then carries as a PRESENT location rendering `[0:0]` and, worse, satisfies the attach-if-missing law so no real enclosing span can ever replace it. `Property.DeclarationSpans` is the one such collection a location is read from, and `Property.FirstDeclarationSpan` (`Count == 0 ? null : [0]`) is its ONE reader — used by the generic zero-argument property access and its async twin, the top-level property demand and its async twin, the planned loop temporary (`LoopTempPlan.DeclarationSpan`), `KatLangEngine`'s `MinDecimalPlaces` / `MaxDecimalPlaces` anchors, and `ImportSite.OfProperty`. A spanless property is the ordinary case, not an anomaly (a deconstruction's hoisted `$deconstruct$N` source, a module's locationless import view, a prelude or host-operation binding, a host-built node), so its refused invocation stays spanless and takes whatever legitimate location the current document later attaches. `AbsentSourceLocationTests` pins the invariant on the structured `KatLangError.Span` and the rendered diagnostic across the deconstruction, planner, host-built, imported, sync and async paths, and fails the build on any new `Spans.*OrDefault(` in `src/`. Human coordinates and source-text OFFSETS are different concepts and stay separate: `Token.Position`/`Token.Length` are the UTF-16 offset model, and `Token.Span` is DERIVED from them on every read (`[Line:Column, Line:Column + Length)`; no token spans a line break, so the end column is exactly `Column + Length`; the end-of-file token and the parser's zero-length `Bad` marker are empty spans at their position, while a lexer `Bad` token covers its real width — two columns for an unexpected surrogate pair). The lexer's diagnostics are the offending token's own span; the parser positions every diagnostic at a token extent or at the hull of the tokens it names (`Parser.ReportErrorAt` / `ReportErrorAcross` / `ReportErrorFrom`), never at a width-1 fiction past the text. The attach-if-missing law is exactly the Task 3a law under nullable semantics (`Evaluator.AtSpanIfMissing`: inner span present → keep it; inner absent and outer present → attach the outer; both absent → stay absent) with no default-value check. `KatLangError` carries the ONE structured `Span` and no flattened `StartLine`/`StartColumn`/`EndLine`/`EndColumn` channel (a removed public surface; the same for `SourceSpan`'s former `StartLineNumber`/`EndColumn` record members and the `(int line, int column)` semantic-model queries, which take a `SourcePosition`: `FindResolutionAt`, `FindPropertyAt`, `FindScopeAt`, `GetVisibleSymbolsAt`; a site contains its first column and not the column past its last character). An editor mapping to a zero-based, end-exclusive range (Monaco) converts the BASE only (`Line - 1`, `Column - 1`, or none for Monaco's own 1-based ranges) and never adds one to the end again. FRAME-SIZE DISCIPLINE is the one implementation consequence: a span is a 16-byte value (20 as `SourceSpan?`), and reading it eagerly in a recursive frame — a hidden return buffer plus an argument or initializer copy per site — measurably fattened the parser, loader, front-end, and evaluator frames whose 1 MiB envelopes are calibrated (up to +38% for the parser's worst shapes and +31% for the loader, which crossed its `MaxTraversalDepth` gate). Every calibrated recursive frame therefore holds no span temporaries: the parser builds span-carrying nodes and diagnostics in `[MethodImpl(NoInlining)]` leaf helpers (`NumAt`, `ResolveAt`, `BinaryFrom`, `CallFrom`, `ReportErrorAt`, … — `Parser.LeafFrame`), the module loader and the front-end rewriters copy nodes with `with { Child = … }` so the record copy constructor carries the span, and the evaluator's dispatch arms position errors lazily (`WithSpanOf`, `WithPreferredSpanOf`, `ReserveSequenceCaptureAtRows`, `AtFirstSpanIfMissing`, `CalleeResolutionFailure`, `EvalParamCountedOf`/`EvalResolveCountedOf`) so the frame carries a node reference, not a span copy; the measured envelopes after this discipline are at or below their pre-Task-3b values in both configurations. Lean has no counterpart: source locations are C# diagnostic metadata. `SourceLocationTests`, `SourceLocationPublicSurfaceTests` (consumer compile probes: the retired spellings fail to compile with the named errors, the new surface compiles), `TokenTests`, `LexerDiagnosticSpanTests`, `ParserDiagnosticSpanTests`, `RootStrayCloserRecoveryTests`, `Utf16LexerContractTests`, `ModuleCoordinateSpaceTests`, `AbsentSourceLocationTests`, the `AstStructuralDepthProcessTests` and `ConcurrencyDifferentialTests` stack envelopes.
ABSENCE IS FOR WHAT CANNOT BE POINTED AT, NOT FOR A PATH THAT FORGOT TO POINT (September 2026). The law's other half: a WRITTEN slot is precisely locatable, so its failure must carry its location. `EvalExplicitSequenceValueExprSlots` (and its `MIRROR OF` async twin) is the written-slot dispatcher used by list literals and by nested groups inside them, and only its two UNFOLDING arms — a nested `Expr.Capture` and a zero-argument `Expr.AlgorithmExpr` — bypass `EvalCounted`, which positions every arm it owns. Every other element kind fell through to `EvalCounted` and was located, so exactly the block-shaped elements lost their position: `[1, { }, 3]` reported `Algorithm has no defined output.` with NO span, while the same element in a parenthesized group (`(1, { }, 3)`, which reaches `EvalCounted`'s own Capture arm) was located at the block. Both arms now apply the very rule `EvalCounted` applies to the same node — `WithPreferredSpanOf`: the written node's own span, else its first positioned output row — so the list and the group agree exactly, and `AtSpanIfMissing` keeps a span the inner failure already carries, so a nested slot reports at its own precise location rather than at the enclosing one (`[1, [2, { }], 3]` and `(1, [2, { }])` both name the inner `{ }`). Diagnostic POSITION only: error kinds, structured payloads, rendered messages, values, and emitted counts are unchanged, genuine absence is still absence (a host-built element with no span of its own stays unpositioned rather than borrowing the list's), and Lean models no source locations at all. `AbsentSourceLocationTests` § 7.

## Structural depth and parser budgets

Structural AST depth is a C#-only host-safety policy: every entry point that hands a preconstructed or parsed AST to recursive consumers (evaluator `Run*`, `ParseSyntax`, front-end elaboration, the standalone `ParameterDetector.Detect` / `ImplicitArgumentResolver.Resolve` / `ModuleLoader.Elaborate` boundaries, `SemanticModelBuilder`) must run the iterative `AstStructuralPreflight` FIRST, and a new AST node variant must be added to its child enumeration (the `Expr` half, `TryGetExprChild`, is compiler-exhaustive over the closed hierarchy; any other unknown node kind fails loudly; the variant-owned `Algorithm` collections are enumerated through the internal total accessors, so every variant contributes exactly the collections it owns). The shared `AstWalker.VisitExpr` switch statement keeps a fail-loud guard for the same reason — an unhandled variant would be silently skipped by every walker subclass — and `AstWalkerTraversalTests` pins every declared `Expr` variant's child visits. Two non-raisable tiers: fat-frame consumers (evaluation, front-end elaboration incl. the standalone detector/resolver entry points, semantic modeling) use `EvaluationLimits.MaxSupportedAstDepth`; the small-framed walker-class raw-syntax passes and the measured module-loader traversal use `AstStructuralPreflight.RawSyntaxMaxAstDepth` (the loader also bounds nested module loads CUMULATIVELY against that ceiling — a load site's own traversal depth counts against the module it loads). `MaxAstDepth` may only lower the evaluation tier. Pure-expression spines (unary/binary/comparison-chain/index/list and the sequence joins) must stay ITERATIVE in `Eval`/`EvalCounted` (`EvalExpressionSpineCounted`, classified by the ONE `Evaluator.IsExpressionSpineNode`, which the preflight's own spine predicate and the loop planner's dispatch accounting mirror) — the ceiling assumes they consume no proportional stack, and the 384 KiB-thread subprocess pin enforces it. That pin also holds the RECURSIVE pre-evaluation validation walk, so the `AstWalker.VisitExpr` dispatch frame and the validation walker's override stay LOOP-FREE (child loops live in `NoInlining` helpers): tiered PGO compiles a method with loops as instrumented Tier-0 code, whose Release frames (~1.4 KB per level) overflowed the pin while Debug code never did (S3, October 2026; `AstStructuralDepthTests.ValidationWalkDispatchFrames_ContainNoLoops` pins the IL in every configuration). Every spine kind shares ONE dispatch arm in `Eval`, `EvalCounted` and `EvalCountedAsync`, because a Debug frame holds a struct return temporary per call site and those frames recur once per algorithm boundary (`AstStructuralDepthProcessTests.NearBoundaryShapes_*`). Diagnostic expression-name rendering must stay ITERATIVE and OUTPUT-BOUNDED (`ExprNameRenderer`): the evaluator gates accept arbitrarily long internal join/spread chains, so no diagnostic/optimizer path may render an expression recursively. PARSER recursion budgets are CUMULATIVE across grammar mechanisms: `Parser.MaxNestingDepth` is one weighted per-parse counter that never resets inside a nested container/chain/pattern/call (heavy productions carry calibrated surcharges), its per-shape capacities are proven on 1 MiB threads with ≥2x margin, and a nested module's parse starts with the loader's live-frame stack debt (`Parser.ParseSyntax(source, initialNestingDebt)`) — never add a parser mechanism with its own independent budget or reset. Every charge is SCOPED (September 2026): a base level is entered through `Parser.EnterNestingLevel` and a heavy delimited production — which also registers the closer it is now owed (`IsOwedCloser`) — through `Parser.EnterDelimitedProduction`, each returning a `readonly struct` disposed from a `using` that releases exactly what its entry charged (a refused enter leaves counters untouched before the budget diagnostic aborts the parse); never reintroduce a hand-balanced `try`/`finally` around the counters. `ParserScopedStateTests` pins that recovery paths release both the budget and the owed closer. Parser lookahead over a token run must perform linear total traversal work, including repeated declaration and recovery checks; do not repeatedly rescan growing prefixes or remaining suffixes. The same holds across NESTED candidates: the clause-head confinement check at every call level of a nested call reads one per-parse delimiter-region memo indexed by token position (`Parser.TryCloseDelimiterRegion`) instead of rescanning the levels inside it, and the memo's storage work, reads, and writes are charged to the traversal count (KL-03, `ParserNestingDepthTests`). The walks share a window that can be reused once the cursor passes its entries (`Parser.PrepareRegionMemo`). When a forward walk needs more space, the window grows to cover the opener or double its previous capacity, whichever is larger, up to the end of the stream. A query before a live window can widen it to the full stream to preserve earlier verdicts. `ParserTraversalObservations` counts token visits, memo reads and writes, slots allocated and initialization work (slots allocated, copied and cleared). Pin complexity with deterministic traversal observations. The same rule binds walker-class passes over intentionally SHARED structure: an `AstWalker` subclass whose effective `VisitExplicitParameterDeclaration` and `VisitCollectMarker` hooks are both unused (including inherited behavior) must opt out of the base per-declaration loop (`VisitsExplicitParameterDeclarations => false`, pinned library-wide by `AstWalkerDeclarationVisitPolicyTests`), because the N helpers of a wide assignment deconstruction share ONE N-declaration list and a walk that examines it once per helper is O(N²) (K2-R2: the load-elaboration guard). This is distinct from runtime `MaxDepth` and has no Lean counterpart. PLANNED-LOOP HEADROOM (final audit, September 2026): the loop planner runs INSIDE user recursion on the sync stack with frames the invocation-depth budget does not model, so `LoopExprPlan.TryBuildLoopExprPlan` probes `RuntimeHelpers.TryEnsureSufficientExecutionStack` before planning (insufficient headroom is an ordinary planner fallback to the generic path, reason `InsufficientStackFallbackReason`) and `EvalLoopExprPlan` probes it at every planned unary/binary frame — inside `EvalLoopUnaryPlan`/`EvalLoopBinaryPlan`, the per-kind helpers of its compiler-exhaustive switch expression — (insufficient headroom is the structured `EvaluationStackExhausted`, never a host stack overflow); `EvaluationLimitsProcessTests.PlannedLoopUnderRecursion_IsStructurallyBounded_InSubprocess` pins the class in a subprocess and requires any success to equal the generic path's value.

FRONT-END STACK RESERVE PROBES (audit finding F1, November 2026; C#-only host-stack safety, Q-09a, no Lean counterpart): the structural gates and the parser budget above bound a tree's SHAPE for a front end that starts near the top of its thread, but a compile can start on a deep stack. A deferred module region's selected branch compiles inside `DeferredModuleRegion.CompileLoadedBody`, and its modules parse inside `ModuleLoader.FetchAndSpliceAsync`, on whatever stack evaluation has already used when the branch is selected; when nothing suspends, that is the evaluator's own stack, and a valid in-budget program killed the host process there. Measurement ruled out a depth-scaled headroom gate: stack per level differs by shape and build, the same tree needs more stack at a deeper base, flat chains charge no parser nesting at all, and exhaustion can surface with no managed frame, so a wrong constant is a process crash. Instead EVERY front-end recursion cycle probes the runtime's stack reserve once per level through the ONE probe, `FrontEndStack` (`Ensure`, whose throw is out of line, and `HasReserve`): the parser at its `EnterNestingLevel` chokepoint (and `NormalizeOpenExpr`, which recurses over an iteratively parsed open-target chain), the post-parse validation walk (`PreEvaluationValidationWalker.VisitAlgorithm`/`VisitExpr` — the FIRST recursive consumer of a flat operator, postfix or dot chain), every recursion of `ParameterDetector`, `ImplicitArgumentResolver` and `PropertyExposureResolver` including its dependency-summary walk, and the module loader's walks, load-bearing pre-scan and import-view rewrite. The probe's control exception is converted at exactly these boundaries and reaches no other route: `Parser.ParseSyntaxCore` (the chokepoint reports through the budget diagnostic `NestingTooDeep`; the post-parse walk's catch reports the same code with the placeholder root, so a nested module classifies as `ModuleNestingTooDeep`), `FrontEndPipeline.FinalizeElaboration` and `CompileLoadedBody` (`ModuleElaborationStackExhausted`; the pipeline reports into the operation's bag, never the provisional stage), `Evaluator.PreEvaluationValidationError` (`EvaluationStackExhausted`), and the loader's established catches. A probe changes nothing for a run with enough stack — same values, diagnostics, order, charges, caching and allocations; it can only refuse a run that would have needed the runtime's last reserve. WHERE a probe fires depends on the host, the build and the stack already in use, and is never promised; no configured limit influences it (Q-09b) and no test pins it. A refusal is never a clause non-match, is never memoized, publishes nothing (the region stays retryable: the same tree compiles on a run with more stack), is outside `IsResourceLimit` (Q-71), and is terminal for its run through `RetainRequiredSourceFailure`. A NEW recursive front-end walk must probe at the head of its recursion (an existing method — no wrapper frame, span temporary or local in a calibrated frame, and any DAG memo check stays inside the frame), or record why it need not: its depth is bounded by a small constant, or on every route that reaches it a probed walk with larger frames has already covered the same tree at the same stack base, reaching every shared node no shallower (check route by route — the validation walk looked covered until flat chains showed nothing recursive ran before it; and a pass elaborates a spliced module root once, at its FIRST reach, so visit order matters: the exposure pass visits properties before opens, unlike the detector and the resolver, and on a chain of modules that each open the next and also load it deep inside a property it alone stacks every module's nesting, which its own probes and the dependency-summary probes it drives stop; removing only the exposure file's probes has no independent crash witness — `DeferredCompileStackProcessTests.ModuleChainSweep_ProbeChild`). Shared helpers that the evaluator or the editor also call carry no probe, because nothing would convert it there; they run from probed frames, over depths the structural gates bound. `DeferredCompileStackProcessTests` sweeps every route (synchronous download, cached module, deep module text) and fifteen shapes across thread sizes, the evaluator window on a 1 MiB thread, the public engine route and a thread-pool thread in child processes. The thread-size sweeps require a compile and accept only structured refusals. Deep shapes also require a compile refusal when the requested stack sizes are honored. The flat comparison chain is a single wide node, so smaller stacks can hit the evaluator backstop before the front end is reached.

EVALUATOR NAVIGATION RESERVE PROBES (F1 and its HOME review, November 2026; C#-only host-stack safety, Q-09a, no Lean counterpart): two evaluator navigations recurse once per LINK of a written chain and can start deep on the evaluator's stack — a deferred branch evaluated deep in recursion, or a lexical lookup that consults the opens from wherever evaluation has got to — so the structural gate (3 units per dot link) bounds their length but not the stack left when they start. Each carries the evaluator's established per-link reserve check (`RuntimeHelpers.TryEnsureSufficientExecutionStack` → `EvaluationStackExhausted`, never `FrontEndStack`, whose control exception is converted only at the front-end boundaries): the argumentless dot-receiver navigation (`Evaluator.ResolveDotReceiver`), and the `open`-target navigation (`Evaluator.ResolveAlgForOpen` ↔ `Evaluator.ResolveOpenPropAccess`, probed in `ResolveOpenPropAccess` BEFORE it recurses on the receiver; the `OpenStructure` probe runs only after the descent returns, which is why a long dotted `open` target inside deep recursion once killed the process). One probe per link, on one method of a mutually recursive pair, at the head of an existing frame. Where either fires depends on the host, the build and the stack in use and is never promised, so a maximal ~100-link chain may be refused on a 1 MiB thread; a refusal is a resource limit, terminal for its run. `Evaluator.ResolveAlg` and `ResolveDotCallAlgorithm` dispatch one node and do not recurse per link, and the open lookup over the scope parent chain (`LookupOpensInParentChain`) is a loop. `EvaluatorOpenChainStackProcessTests` sweeps the open family across recursion depth, link count and thread size, inside deferred compiles, and under CPU load, in child processes.

NO STACKED REWRITES (B3-5★, November 2026): implicit-argument resolution selects every inferred signature before it rewrites anything ([`ownership-and-lookup.md` § Signature selection]), so no rewrite ever processes another body in its middle: the former on-demand processing of a pending sibling — the one place two front-end rewrites stacked — and its demand-depth budget (ten units per active demand, `AstDepthLimitExceeded` beyond it) are deleted with the processing order. Each phase stays within the one calibrated traversal of the tree it walks.
EXPOSURE SETTLEMENT RUNS ON AN EXPLICIT STACK (September 30 2026): the exposure pass settles a member read (`P.A`, or `A` through `open P`) by charging the member's requirement seed, whose own member reads it settles in turn — as deep as a CHAIN of such reads, which source makes arbitrarily long with no deep syntax at all (every link is a sibling), so no structural gate bounds it. `PropertyExposureResolver.PendingResolution` therefore runs each nested settlement as a heap frame — a C# iterator yielding the nested frame it needs next, driven by ONE explicit-stack loop (`Run`) that resumes it exactly where the recursion would have returned — so the evaluation order, every recorded level read, every in-progress cycle cut and every observation are the recursive form's, and a chain of any length settles on a 1 MiB thread. Formerly about 470 siblings reading each other through `open` (about 590 through dotted reads, fewer in a debug build) overflowed a 1 MiB thread and killed the host process, on HEAD as well; a before/after differential of every property's `Exposure`, `RequiredAncestorParameters` and `CaptureRequirements` over 1,691 programs is identical. Only the stack changed: settlement still revisited a chain per level (quadratic for a long chain: N(N + 3) frames for N opened links at a parameterized owner, counted by `RequiredAncestorSettlementFrames`) until the run-scoped settlement share of November 2026 (§ EXPOSURE-OPEN-1, SETTLEMENT SHARE). The summary channel formerly carried each opened link's provider seed on the reference that read it, rebuilt and re-serialized per enclosing link (cubic; and a cycle of them never terminated); a pending reference carries no provider now, and that channel was deleted in November 2026 (§ EXPOSURE-OPEN-1 below, which also records the summary channel's depth history). `ExposureSettlementDepthTests`.

## Shared-DAG guarantee

B3-2★ summary representation (October 2026; FORMAL-02, no language change): immutable Q8 work is shared only after its contextual dependencies are accounted for. Effect-free completed computations share by source body reference, with binder-set and authoritative parameter agreement for branches; shared argument bundles preserve context-relative reads and one completed tail seed. A context-bearing branch recipe may use one memo-local equation representative only for the identical binding-free subtree under the exact same parent and fact source, with matching authoritative availability, providers and name facts; corresponding self-targets preserve kind and provider identity. This does not merge actual structural keys, branch occurrences, declaring/captured owners or runtime activations. Other contextual summaries retain exact fact/scope keys, and no partial projection is a completed publication. Every summary is computed in a completed structural context (§ EXPOSURE-OPEN-1, ONE SUMMARY MODE): the effect-free share is a cache of that one mode, never a contextless walk. These caches live for one summary memo and never cross the frozen continuation boundary. Original sharing, allocation and semantic controls remain binding; see `static-structure-query-contracts-2026-10.md` and `experimental/features/alias-unify-1-b32-exposure-sharing-2026-10-29.md`.

Host ASTs may legally contain SHARED acyclic subtrees (a node referenced from several parents; cycles stay preflight-rejected), and module elaboration itself splices one cached module algorithm at every load site of the same URL. The front-end passes (`ParameterDetector`, `ImplicitArgumentResolver`, `PropertyExposureResolver`, `PropertyDependencyGraphBuilder`, `ModuleLoader` incl. its load-bearing pre-scan, plus the `LoadElaborationGuard`/`FinalPropertyExposure` helper walks) are DAG-SAFE: every walk carries a run-local REFERENCE-IDENTITY memo per constant-context region, so work is bounded by distinct reachable nodes rather than root-to-node paths wherever semantics permit, and rewritten output preserves sharing within one context. Semantics stay tree-equivalent (grace weights accumulate per semantic occurrence as exact unbounded integers, summed once by Q7 with each shared node's exact occurrence multiplicity — PAR-06, X-49); the deliberate per-NODE behaviors are diagnostic multiplicity within one diagnostic context and load-site identity within one constant loader context. Load-bearing loader keys include effective live traversal depth because near-ceiling admission is path-sensitive; the same node reached at two such depths intentionally splits. The `ModuleLoader` memo checks live INSIDE the calibrated walk frames — never add a wrapper method or extra async state machine to its recursion spine. A new memoized traversal must keep its explicit variant switch (the memo wrapper adds no dispatch); pinned by `FrontEndDagComplexityTests` and the `AstGraphFuzzTests` front-end shared-vs-cloned campaign. SHARED-DAG GUARANTEE: a legal shared acyclic AST is processed with work bounded by distinct nodes × distinct semantic regions, and traversal-path multiplicity affects neither results, nor diagnostics, nor asymptotic work. The pre-evaluation signature-validity and clause-head checks of a host AST that shares pattern subtrees (the singleton rule, one collector per level, repeated names, the explicit-parameter anchor) are linear in the distinct pattern nodes, with the equivalent tree's verdict, rule order and first witness (`SharedPatternDagValidationTests`); so are the parameter-name sets of static structure, open-provider admission, the lifting-recovery rewrite (which keeps the input's sharing), contract comparison and the library walkers' clause-head visits, while the per-occurrence parameter APIs and their occurrence-list readers stay per path by contract (`SharedPatternDagConsumerTests`). END TO END (November 2026): ordinary front-end entry takes source, but a parsed deferred placeholder retains public nested pattern memberships also held by its raw body: a host edit before materialization can send a shared signature through parameter detection, joint selection, rewrite and exposure on Evaluator.RunAsync. Explicit detector ownership names and resolver/region name-set and first-occurrence consumers use expand-once walks; a closed detector list retains its original pattern shape. The selected explicit and formula-lifting deferred-signature routes are bounded by distinct pattern nodes/edges per composition/binding context, plus existing selector work: a monotone name merge expands each group once, and synthesized argument value expressions preserve DAG sharing within their fixed name/kind mapping. This includes an unused repeated-capture callee and a formula H = G + 1 with no occurrence-sized published result; evaluation still consumes every semantic occurrence when H is called. Runtime diagnostic and declaring-scope name projections use completed output ranges (ParameterPattern.SelectCapturesSkippingBarrenNodes), preserving order and duplicates with O(V+E+K) work for K selected occurrences. The selected small runtime reports without provenance notes and capture-free member navigation are O(V+E); existing cycle-root note enrichment is unchanged. Reports quoting a pattern (shape mismatches, rendered callable signatures) and eager editor property metadata remain bounded by their expanded rendered output R and per-occurrence entries. Scope-name arrays retain K internal names as well; that is not necessarily a public-output lower bound. Successful-call activation, loop/optimizer preparation and other occurrence projections remain outside these selected-route guarantees: no global linearity claim (SharedSignatureDagEndToEndTests). Spliced module content no longer multiplies the regions: a loaded module is rooted at the prelude and elaborated ONCE per program whatever scope splices it (Q-31 H-P + Q-32 I-U, § Source loading and deferred modules), and every cache-hit splice still charges the view's elaborated weight against the aggregate source ceiling (SPLICES CHARGE THE AGGREGATE), so module content stays bounded by `MaxAggregateSourceLength`. Region keys are the minimal complete semantic context, run-local, node/scope identity by reference and name sets by content: detector branch bodies (parent scope, binder set, captured names, reporting mode), and any body several paths reach (its binding footprint, BINDING REGIONS below); resolver nested algorithms — property values, block literals, open targets, branch bodies alike — (the signature SNAPSHOT of the algorithm's FREE reference names in the visible map, the closed binder specification for a branch body, reporting mode); exposure (the region's final visible summaries; across parents, `SharedExposureRegion`); summary channel (node, or body plus binder set); the B3-5★ signature analysis (its own region key, `AnalysisRegionKey`, refining the rewrite's); loader (load context plus effective live depth); the editor's `Semantics/SemanticModelBuilder` (node plus scope frame; algorithms additionally plus a branch's binder table). Family-worded diagnostics are stored as templates and replayed per family sharing a body (`same node + same region → reused; same node + different region → independently processed`). Inferred signatures are selected before anything is rewritten (B3-5★, [`ownership-and-lookup.md` § Signature selection]), so sibling declaration order is not observable, and a lifting cycle is completed simultaneously, never ordered, and refused only when it does not settle; the signature analysis memoizes regions exactly as finely as the rewrite (`AnalysisRegionKey`) and maps a path-sensitive context it served from another context's analysis to that counterpart (`SignatureKeyAliases`), while the rewrite records each region it serves from another context's processing as a REGION ALIAS (`StaticStructureOperation.AliasResolvedRegion`): every resolved view and pending cell below it is read through its counterpart, and a served context's completed facts are its own (contextual identity, receiver, open and providers) over the counterpart's processed view, while every context below it is a lazy projection of its counterpart (its own key, re-based selections and declaring identity, and the view exposure gave that occurrence) — never published once per path; retargeting walks below a served context only where exposure copied its occurrence. Explicit exceptions, occurrence-oriented by design: the semantic model analyzes a body once per family that declares its own binders and once per binding region across parents (BINDING REGIONS below); the loader splits by depth near the ceiling.

PUBLIC PATTERN HELPERS ARE STACK-SAFE (November 2026; C#-only host-AST safety and implementation-only, NO semantic change and no Lean counterpart). A host may hand a public pattern helper a pattern far deeper than any structural gate admits, and none of them recurses on the caller's stack. Beside `ParameterPattern.Captures` / `FlattenCaptures` and `Pattern.BoundNames`: the DISPLAY of a structural parameter pattern — `DisplayName` of `SequenceValueParameterPattern`, `ListValueParameterPattern` and `UnpackingParameterPattern`, and with it the record-generated `ToString`, which prints `DisplayName` — is written into one builder with an explicit stack (`ParameterPattern.StructuralDisplayName`; the per-level join it replaces also copied every nested text once per enclosing level); and the CLAUSE-HEAD helpers `Pattern.RequiresConditionalClauseSemantics`, `Pattern.TryGetOrdinaryClauseParams` and `Algorithm.ElaborateClauseGroup` / `ElaborateClauseDefinition` are iterative. Text, records, names and verdicts are those of the recursion they replace, so every parsed program is unchanged (host-built lists no parser writes keep their answers too: a null item of a structural parameter pattern still fails its display with `NullReferenceException`, a null items list still fails with `ArgumentNullException` as before, and a clause head whose own argument list is null is still an exception, while a null item or a nested null list inside a clause head still reads as "no ordinary pattern", like a literal). On a host-built head that SHARES groups: (1) the Boolean query is `Pattern.IsLiteralFreeClauseHead`, a walk that expands each group once, stops at the first literal and builds nothing — linear in the head's distinct nodes and edges; (2) the name list is output-sensitive — a group's names are produced at its first expansion and copied for every later occurrence, so the work is distinct nodes + edges + names returned; (3) `ElaborateClauseGroup` decides ordinary-versus-family with the linear query first, so a literal-holding head is refused without being expanded, and then converts an ordinary head into ONE RECORD PER OCCURRENCE. That expansion is deliberate: the elaborated `ParameterPatterns` are public, and record equality compares an items list by reference, so two occurrences of one shared group are two distinct, unequal records exactly as in a parsed tree; keeping the head's sharing would make them one record, which a host can observe. Its work is therefore the size of the tree the head denotes (per-occurrence output, like `Parameters` / `Params`). (4) Bare forwarding's branch-head contracts (`ImplicitArgumentResolver.BareForwardingSources.OfBranchHead`) read the converted patterns only as contracts (`ParameterPattern.ContractComparer`) and never hand them on, so there a shared group is converted once (`Pattern.TryCreateOrdinaryClauseParameterPatternKeepingSharing`: a call-local memo for the whole head that also remembers the groups found to hold a literal); the shared result has the per-occurrence result's contract, contract hash and captures. Display is per occurrence by definition, so its work is the size of the text. Nothing is attached to a pattern: a host that mutates a retained list reads the current answer on the next call. THE RESOLVER ROUTE: a host can edit a clause-head list of a parsed deferred body before it is first compiled, and the edit reaches bare forwarding's contracts on `Evaluator.RunAsync`; a head deeper than the structural gate never does (the run's admission refuses it, `AstDepthLimitExceeded`). Not covered: on that route `ImplicitArgumentResolver.BranchBinderParameterPatterns` and `FrontEndRegionKeys.RenderBranchHead` still read a shared clause head once per path (measured), so no end-to-end bound is claimed for a shared head inside a deferred body. `AstStructuralDepthTests` (200,000-level pins of each helper), `StackSafePatternHelperTests` (the former recursion kept verbatim as an oracle over the parser corpus and over generated trees and shared DAGs; read budgets on shared heads; the deferred host route).

NATIVE RECORD EQUALITY AND HASHING ARE STACK-SAFE (November 2026; C#-only host-AST safety and implementation-only, NO semantic change, no public API change and no Lean counterpart). The compiler synthesizes a record's `Equals` and `GetHashCode` as one call per field through `EqualityComparer<TField>.Default`, so a field whose type is itself a record recursed on the caller's stack, once per level. A host builds such chains as deep as it likes (the structural gate applies to a run, not to a record), and 200,000 levels overflowed the process on every native route alike: `Equals(T)`, `Equals(object)`, the base-typed `Equals`, `==`, `!=`, `GetHashCode`, the default comparer, and a `HashSet` or `Dictionary` keyed by the record (reproduced on a 1 MiB thread before the change, Debug build: equality from 5,000 levels, hashing from 20,000). THE RECORDS CONCERNED are exactly those with a record-valued field that can reach its own kind again: the ten `Expr` variants that hold an expression or an algorithm directly — `Unary`, `Binary`, `Comparison` (`First`), `Index` (`Target`), `SequenceConstruct`, `SequenceSpread`, `DotCall` (`Target` and `LexicalFallback`), `Grace`, `AlgorithmExpr`, `Call` (`Function`) — `Algorithm.Alias` (`Parent`, `Target`), and `ScopeCtx` (`Parent`; its equality was hand-written already, and recursive). `Algorithm.User` and `Algorithm.Conditional` reach only the scope chain through `Parent`; they are included so that one operation's memo also covers the scopes many algorithms share. These fourteen records declare `Equals(T)` and `GetHashCode()` themselves and delegate to ONE iterative walk, `RecordGraphEquality`, over an explicit stack of indexed continuation frames; every other native route is still synthesized and ends in those two members. THE CONTRACT IS THE GENERATED ONE: reference identity, else the same record kind and every field in declaration order through the same default comparers and, for the hash, the same multiplier — the base record's part is still the compiler's (`base.Equals`, `base.GetHashCode`). So source spans and metadata are compared as before, a list or an `OutputBundle` compares by its own equality (reference identity unless a host type defines another: two lists with equal content are still two different records), a `RuntimeStateSlot` is still transparent, `ScopeCtx` still ignores its runtime `Owner` and `Activation`, a `with` copy equals its source, and a record's hash is the value the synthesized member returned in the same process (`ScopeCtx` keeps `HashCode.Combine(Parent, Opens, Properties, Parameters)`). Fields are read in the generated order — a field only after everything before it compared equal — so a host collection with its own `Equals` is asked exactly when it was. NOT LANGUAGE EQUALITY: `Result.ValueComparer`, `ParameterPattern.ContractComparer` and the pattern match-equivalence index are separate and untouched. RECORDS THAT NEEDED NO CHANGE: `Pattern`, `ParameterPattern` (with `ParameterDeclaration`) and `Result` keep their structural children in LISTS, which generated equality compares by reference and never enters, so depth never mattered there and two independently built structures are unequal; `ComparisonLink`, `Property` and `CondBranch` only ENTER an expression or an algorithm, one synthesized frame above the iterative walk. SHARED NODES: within one top-level operation each ordered pair of record references is compared at most once and each record is hashed at most once, so the work is proportional to the distinct records (pairs) and their fields, never to the paths that reach them (the generated members did the same work once per path: a 60-level diamond never finished). A shared graph equals the tree it denotes, at the cost of their distinct pairs. Recursive-child classification is compiler-exhaustive over `Expr` and `Algorithm`. The memo includes shallow leaf records too; the root's first leaf result is kept inline so a repeated leaf needs no allocation, then copied into the memo if a later child descends. The memo is keyed by reference identity, allocated with the stack at the first descent that needs them, and discarded with the call: nothing is stored on a record, so a caller-owned collection edited between two operations is read afresh by the second. ONE-CHILD ROOT: a root with ONE record-valued field whose child holds only leaves or nulls needs neither — it asks that child's own `Equals` / `GetHashCode`, which is the stateless leaf walk (one bounded native call, the generated field order, a leaf held by both of the child's fields still read once), and a single root edge leaves no second branch that could meet one of the child's pairs again (`RecordGraphEquality.ChildrenAreLeaves`; a leaf has no record-valued field, so no cycle hides there). COST ON ORDINARY INPUT: a record whose record-valued fields hold only leaves or nulls, and a one-child root over such a record, need no traversal state; equality also skips identical child references, while hashing must still traverse their descendants; any wider or deeper shape pays for the stack and the memo (`NativeRecordEqualityBenchmarks`, Release on .NET 11.0.0, Windows x64, November 2026: the one-child nested shape compares in 31 ns and hashes in 32 ns without allocating, formerly 90 ns / 74 ns with 960 B / 824 B; 19 nested records still compare in about 0.9 µs with 2.75 KB instead of 70 ns, and a 1,000-level chain in about 33 µs with 180 KB instead of 3.6 µs). The library itself keys AST nodes by reference; its two native uses are the diagnostic `Evaluator.TryGetAlgorithmNameInScope` and the exposure summary memo's tuple key over an `Algorithm`. Equality can skip a parent that is the same scope object, but hashing a non-null parent still traverses that chain. CYCLES: a record-valued field is assigned from an existing record, so no public construction makes a record reach itself through such fields; host reference cycles run through caller-owned lists (the structural preflight's contract), which records compare as lists, so they were and stay shallow. A cycle forged by reflection had no answer before (it overflowed the stack) and is refused with `InvalidOperationException`, never given an invented verdict. ELSEWHERE: the record TEXT of these records (RAW RECORD TEXT, below) and `EvalError.WithContext`, a public record outside the AST (§ The evaluation-error taxonomy, C# RECORD OPERATIONS ON CONTEXT FRAMES), are handled separately. NOT COVERED: internal plan records keep their synthesized members and are built from admitted trees. The hash FORMULA is unchanged, including its weakness: a record that holds one child in two fields folds that child in twice, and the multiplier plus one is even, so about 32 such levels cancel the child out of the hash (collisions, never a wrong equality). `NativeRecordEqualityTests` (the synthesized contract written over reflection as the oracle, validated against the generated members before the change: every verdict and hash over generated trees and shared DAGs, every field mutated in turn, and the parser corpus; the exact inventory of hand-written records and of records on a record-valued cycle; 200,000-level pins; exact per-node work counts on shared DAGs; collection contracts), `AstStructuralDepthProcessTests.DeepHostAsts_NativeRecordEqualityAndHashing_AreStackSafe_InSubprocess` (the same graphs on a 1 MiB thread in a child process).

RAW RECORD TEXT IS STACK-SAFE (November 2026; C#-only host display and implementation-only, NO semantic change, no public API change and no Lean counterpart). The compiler synthesizes a record's `ToString` as a `PrintMembers` call that appends every public member through that member's own `ToString`, so a record-valued member recursed once per level and copied every nested text once per enclosing level; the compiler's own stack guard turned a deep host-built chain into a catchable `InsufficientExecutionStackException`, so such a record had no text at all. The fourteen records of the paragraph above declare `PrintMembers` themselves and delegate to ONE iterative writer, `AstRecordText`, which writes nested records into the root's builder over an explicit stack of (record, next segment) continuations; `ToString` itself is still synthesized (the root's name and braces). THE LAYOUT IS THE GENERATED ONE: the base record's public members, then the record's own in declaration order, as `Name = value` — computed properties included (`DotCall.EffectiveLexicalFallback`, the `Parameters` / `Params` projections of `Algorithm.User`), a null member as empty text, a collection as that collection prints itself (never entered), every other value through its own `ToString`. TEXT IS PER OCCURRENCE: a child reached through two members is written twice (a dot edge with a stored `LexicalFallback` prints it under `LexicalFallback` and again under `EffectiveLexicalFallback`), so work and size are those of the expanded text — there is no shared-graph bound to give, as there was none before. A cycle through the record-valued members the writer follows (reflection only) is refused with `InvalidOperationException`. A caller-owned collection can also re-enter `ToString` through its own formatter. The writer retains the compiler's stack check at entry, so repeated host re-entry throws `InsufficientExecutionStackException`. ONE INVENTORY: the writer decides by the equality classification (`RecordChildCount`) which member values it writes itself; a record kind it was never taught is an `InvalidOperationException`, never an empty member list; and the records with hand-written text are pinned to be exactly the records with hand-written equality. `NativeRecordTextTests` (the synthesized layout restated over reflection, for every record kind and for nested chains of all of them; the inventory; the forged cycle; 50,000-level renderings), `AstStructuralDepthProcessTests.DeepHostRecords_NativeTextAndErrorMessages_AreStackSafe_InSubprocess` (the deep renderings on a 1 MiB thread in a child process).

BINDING REGIONS ACROSS PARENTS (SHARED-GRAPH-1, November 2026; C# front-end representation and performance, NO semantic change and no Lean counterpart). A body shared by DISTINCT parents (`Left = { S = B }`, `Right = { S = B }`, repeated per level: 3d + 3 algorithms, 2^d paths) used to be copied per path by detection, registered per path by the name-resolved input, walked per path by the exposure summary channel and the alias context of pre-evaluation validation, so a legal graph was refused near depth 10. A body more than one path reaches (`SharedBodyCensus`, taken at admission; a tree has none and pays no region key) is now processed once per BINDING REGION: the contexts whose free names select the same declarations. Detection compares, for every name the body can read from outside it (`RegionFreeNames`, an over-approximation), the owned, opened and open-head bindings of the declaring scope by binding identity (`BindingFootprint`: declaring key and property object, never a displayed value or signature; an unsettled fact keeps the context apart). Owned and opened are separate facts because an inner open sits between them. A served context publishes the one detected view and aliases the context that processed it (`AliasDetectedRegion`, frozen with a continuation); everything below it reads through `DetectedProcessingContext`, memoized by context. Only a CLEAN processing is served: one that reported nothing and left no check waiting for resolution, so a body that reports stays once per reach like its tree, and a provisional walk and everything reaching a deferred region are excluded (their forked contexts are an occurrence's own). The name-resolved registration aliases a supplied body's further contexts to its first one (a family is no level of its branches, so each holder walks them). Exposure serves a shared body from `SharedExposureRegion`: the exact level declaring each free name (final summaries and members), the RELATIVE position of each owner, the level depth, and the nearest level with opens when a name reaches past the direct chain; two parents' parameters at one relative position are one region because its metadata is relative, while their runtime activations stay their own. The summary channel maps a shared body's context to the first one of its `CompletedBodyRegion` (what each free name selects in completed facts); the dot-member provenance finalizer and the editor semantic model visit such a body once per that region (one analysis per binder table), and the open-provider validator once per clean region. Pre-evaluation validation keys a visit by `StaticAliasTargets.RegionOf` (for each alias-target head free in the node, the chain prefix ending at its declaring scope) instead of the whole chain. Bodies keep their contexts: actual keys, selections, declaring identities and diagnostics are unchanged, a served reach is the existing lazy projection, and `ExactContext` of a body shared this way refuses and asks for the occurrence (`Child`). Bound: work is distinct nodes × binding regions. RESIDUAL NECESSARY GROWTH: contexts binding a read name to different declarations stay apart (a level per name gives 2^d binding tuples, which evaluation tells apart), so `control-bindings` keeps its exponential regions and is charged and refused by the static ledger with the unchanged category; the bare-read refinement of that key is not attempted. One-shot standalone analysis (`AnalyzedStaticStructures.Project`, including exposure of a host graph no pass completed) uses the same binding footprint to retain representative facts and lazy occurrence projections. It reuses the immutable projection representation behind an analysis-only wrapper: no AST attachment or Q9 completion, no retained producer or token. Each occurrence keeps its own keys, member/receiver/provider selections and rebased declaring identities; pending and deferred contexts are excluded. Incremental `StandaloneScopeAnalysis` query-source snapshots continue to enumerate their admitted contexts: a later `Inside` must find the declaring context in the frozen query source, so one-shot fact projections cannot be substituted there. Footprint arrays are charged before allocation, including inconclusive attempts, with construction and comparison work and cancellation in the operation ledger. Pinned by `SharedGraphExpansionTests`; measurements in `artifacts/validation/shared-graph-expansion/`.

WIDE CONTEXTS (FE-1, September 2026; C# front-end performance/architecture, NO semantic change and no Lean counterpart). The same guarantee holds along the second axis: a scope that sees W names — a wide owner's parameters, many sibling properties — and contains K nested scopes, properties, branches, or promotions costs O(K + W) context work, never O(K × W). A context is built ONCE and EXTENDED by what a nested scope adds: the resolver's visible signature map (`ImplicitArgumentResolver.SignatureMap`; a deferred branch keeps its snapshot in O(1)), the detector's `ParameterOwnership`, and the collision validator's `ParameterBindings` are PERSISTENT immutable dictionaries that share every inherited entry. Every name-set region key is a CANONICAL id from a run-local `NameSetInterner` — hash-consed big-endian Patricia tries, so equal content is the SAME node whatever order or grouping built it (exact structural identity, never a hash, never a derivation path) — derived from the enclosing context's set plus the names a context adds: detector branch bodies' captured names (`DetectionRun.CapturedNamesId` over `ParameterOwnership.Parent` / `AddedNames`), and the collision validator's visible parameter names (`ContextNames`); union and difference memoize every branch pair they combine, so a context that differs by a few names costs those names. Reference-keyed caches over immutable inputs (a scope level, a parameter map, an algorithm) are caches of pure functions, never region identity. Derived metadata is materialized once: an owner's parameter-name set per resolution (`SummaryMemo.ParameterNamesOf`, shared by every property summary, fixed-point round, and exposure scope — the live `Algorithm.User.Params` projection is never re-read per property), and requirement qualification intersects by scanning the SMALLER side. A promotion's near-miss suggestion only CAPTURES its canonical candidate context (`SuggestionContexts`, derived per scope level and parameter map, opens folded in level by level); the comparison runs when a diagnostic renders the suggestion (`SuggestionQuery.Evaluate`), so a valid program compares no candidate however many names it promotes. Pinned by `WideSemanticContextComplexityTests` (exact counters — `ContextEntriesWritten`, `ContextNamesCanonicalized`, `OwnerParameterNamesMaterialized`, `SummaryQualificationProbes`, `NameSetOperationSteps`, `SuggestionCandidatesExamined` — at two sizes, derivation diamonds that share one region per level, a wide clause-family diamond, a wide deferred case, and a design-independent guard that the allocation K children add does not grow with the context width), `CanonicalNameSetTests`, `SuggestionContextDifferentialTests` (the incremental candidate derivation against a verbatim copy of the per-name collector it replaced), and `DeferredSuggestionProtocolTests`. Outside this guarantee: the editor semantic model's per-scope `ScopeVisibility.Symbols` are independent snapshots by their public contract (K scopes × W visible symbols) — output size, not repeated context work. (Implicit lifting's synthesized arguments were the other such expansion until FE-2 below shared them.)

The FE-1 review also pins work performed before a region-cache hit: resolver signature footprints project only the body's free names and update from immutable map deltas; their canonical `(name, signature identity)` sets decide region equality, never map-version identity. Shared branch patterns use pass-local `BranchContextInterner` metadata: name-set identities are canonical, closed specifications are interned by exact content, and neither is re-read or re-hashed per referencing family. Deferred detection keeps only the immutable binder set. A repeated branch-body summary contributes once per family and summary representative (since November 18 2026; a binding-free body shared by many literal branches is one representative, see EXPOSURE-OPEN-1, DEAD GUARDS AND ONE CONTRIBUTION PER REPRESENTATIVE); sibling-walk shadow metadata is derived once per body and entered context. Pending-reference binding reuses the owner's already-materialized parameter-name set, including its empty/nonempty verdict. Suggestion provider sets combine canonical public-member sets without re-enumerating a wide shared provider for each list. Final receiver confirmations retain immutable member-name snapshots rather than caller-owned AST collections. `Fe1HostileReviewTests` pins these boundaries, actual parameter-pattern reads, retained-note collectibility, concurrent rendering, deep ownership, and wide host/module interactions. Physical `RequiredAncestorParameters` lists remain a separate representation cost, as do `ScopeVisibility.Symbols`.

FAILED-RUN NAME SUGGESTIONS (November 2026). `UnresolvedImplicitParams` and `ArityMismatch` can carry inferred parameters lifted from many bodies. `KatLangError` renders their suggestions in note order. Each message has room for 32,768 candidate comparisons and 32,768 receiver confirmations. A note must fit both remaining allowances in full. A skipped note keeps its ordinary origin, receiver or cycle wording, and a later note can still fit. Charging uses the captured counts even when a suggestion has already been computed, so the public `FromEvalError`, terminal-safe rendering and concurrent renderings agree.

The bounded optimal-string-alignment kernel computes only cells within the accepted diagonal band and stops when the row minimum exceeds the threshold. Each comparison visits at most 448 distance cells. A receiver surface shares its invariant-case spellings across evaluations. The lexical and member thresholds, initial guard and tie rule retain their existing behavior. `ProjectEvaluationOutcome` passes the evaluation token to failure rendering, which checks it on entry, at each inferred note and after formatting. Cancellation during the last suggestion also escapes with that token. `SuggestionRenderingBoundTests` covers the full-table oracle, both policies, the work counters, all three note layouts and cancellation inside rendering through the public sync and async operations.

SHARED IMPLICIT ARGUMENTS (FE-2, September 2026; C# front-end representation/performance, NO semantic change and no Lean counterpart). Implicit lifting rewrites each of K lifted references to a callable with L implicit parameters into a call forwarding L synthesized arguments. Those arguments are a pure function of the callee's parameter patterns and the rewrite region's pinned caller configuration (`ImplicitArgumentResolver.BuildImplicitCallArguments` reads nothing about the reference site), so every lifted reference to one callee in one resolver rewrite region shares ONE immutable synthesized argument bundle (`ResolverWalkMemos.ImplicitArguments`, keyed by the callee's pattern list under the region's pinned context; region-local, never static, so nothing outlives the run or crosses a parse); each reference keeps its OWN call node and callee name, which carry its span and its `ResolutionOrigins` entry, while the shared slots are spanless reads of the caller's own bindings that every call evaluates afresh in its own activation — sharing is syntax, never activation state. The elaborated representation of K references is therefore O(K + L), not K × L, and every later pass processes a shared bundle once per context: the exposure pass rewrites it once per region (`PropertyExposureResolver.RewriteArgumentBundle`) and its summary channel completes an argument bundle's seed once per region and absorbs a large completed seed BY REFERENCE, folding each distinct absorbed seed into a set the first time the set is read (`SummarySeed.AbsorbCompleted`, `CompletedArgumentBundleSeed`); the memoizing walkers — collision and open-provider validation, the provenance finalizer, pre-evaluation validation, the load guard, the semantic model and its module-provenance collector — memoize a call's argument bundle under exactly the context they memoize a node (the assembly-internal `AstWalker.VisitCallArguments` hook; the semantic model keys (bundle, frame, region)); and the structural preflight enumerates a call's argument bundle as ONE weightless grouping child (so `DeferredModuleRegion.RequiresAsyncEvaluation` shares it too), positioning a rejection met at a shared bundle at the slot the slot-by-slot walk reports. The public elaborated AST may therefore share one `OutputBundle` (and its slot nodes) between the argument lists of several `Expr.Call`/`Expr.DotCall` nodes; a bundle is an immutable snapshot and its synthesized slots are immutable records, so a host may observe the alias with `ReferenceEquals` but cannot mutate a synthesized slot through either call (a record `with` expression creates a new node). FE-2 kept this sharing within one owner's rewrite region; FE-3 below shares a bundle across owners whose forwarding inputs are identical. Pinned by `ImplicitArgumentSharingTests` (the reference-identity census of the elaborated graph at four sizes, exact per-pass counters — `ImplicitCallsSynthesized`, `ImplicitArgumentBundlesBuilt`, `ImplicitArgumentSlotsBuilt`, `ExposureArgumentBundleRewrites`, `DependencyArgumentBundleSummaries`, `WalkerCallArgumentSlots`, `SemanticModelCallArgumentSlots`, `StructuralPreflightEdges` — a design-independent allocation guard over the public path, the sharing scope's exactness, per-reference provenance and blame, and runtime independence: seeded draws, host operations, genuine async suspension, the property cache, planned loops, deferred materialization).

The FE-2 hostile review also covers loop-plan construction: repeated lifted calls of one planned local property share their forwarding-shape verdict and first-argument limit span through `LoopTempCallArgumentMemo`, keyed by both the argument bundle and the selected `LoopTempPlan` by reference. The memo belongs only to one plan-construction invocation, spans its output rows and local-property plans, and stores no runtime values or activation state. Each call retains its own plan node, callee, and diagnostic boundary; expression-plan memos and stack-headroom decisions remain separate. The step's ordered state names are projected once for the construction. `ImplicitArgumentSharingHostileReviewTests` pins actual slot inspections, signature separation, span retention, and optimized/generic value and emitted-count parity. This removes repeated forwarding-bundle inspection; other planner costs and logical runtime binding are not covered by the front-end linearity claim.

SHARED IMPLICIT SIGNATURES (FE-3, September 2026; C# front-end representation/performance, NO semantic change and no Lean counterpart). K owners that each lift a callable with L implicit parameters hold K × L LOGICAL bindings — `(owner, slot)` pairs, each bound afresh in its owner's own activations — and until FE-3 they were also K × L PHYSICAL members: the lifted capture records were already shared with the declaring callee, but every owner stored its own freshly copied L-slot `ParameterPatterns` list and its own synthesized bundle, and every later pass re-read, re-inserted, or re-summarized the same L names once per owner (ownership completion, the collision validator's declaration bindings, the exposure pass's owner name sets and summary residuals, the structural preflight's pattern edges, `CallableSignature`'s copied arrays and display text, the open-provider walk, the semantic model's implicit symbols and property metadata). A 20 KB program with K = L = 1,024 sibling owners allocated 2,133 MB to parse (plus 1,429 MB for its editor model), and the same owners nested in distinct parent contexts 4,305 MB. The lifted signature is now a SHARED SIGNATURE TEMPLATE. `ImplicitArgumentResolver.LiftSignature` merges each distinct dependency sequence once per run (`ImplicitSignatureTemplateInterner.LiftedTail`, keyed by the dependencies' pattern-list references) into a FLAT `ImplicitSignatureTemplate` interned by EXACT content — the ordered sequence of pattern RECORD references, so equal spellings over other declarations, orders, or groupings never share; an owner without parameters of its own stores the template itself, an owner whose own names are disjoint from the lifted ones stores its own patterns as a small HEAD composed over the shared flat tail (depth at most one; its facts cost the head), and an owner whose names MEET the lifted ones — an own capture of a lifted name, or (Q-04) an enclosing parameter binding its forwarding reuses — keeps its own order (its capture first, or the reused binding, and the lifted one filtered) as its head composed over the shared tail WITHOUT those names (`ImplicitSignatureTemplateInterner.ComposeExcluding`, November 2026, finding lifting-signatures-fe3-sharing-breaks-on-meeting-names of the 2026-11-21 adversarial performance audit). The per-owner merge it replaced read the owner only through those real collisions — its bound-name set and visited groups were owner state, the tail's captures and shapes were not — so the exclusion is computed once per (shared part, collision name set) by the merge's own filter and interned: owners with equal collisions share one part, different collisions keep different parts, a composed tail's local head is filtered into the owner's head, and only an owner left with none of the shared part stores a flat list of its own. The order and shapes are exactly the per-owner merge's (pinned against an independent oracle).
